# Flash-lane burn queue 2026-09-26

Operator order: burn remaining quota into estate work before reset (tomorrow
afternoon). 5-hour window mechanics untouched. Dispatcher refills to 10
outstanding lanes every fire until 23:55 local or quota death; outstanding
means unchecked Open rows, so a pre-checked row whose work has not landed
mutes the refill count (proof: the B2-222 row sat [x] from self-feed while
its INDEX registrations did not exist until 14:07-14:08Z 2026-09-26,
watch/inbox/burn_wave_20260926/B2_222_hot_living_20260926.md verdict).
Self-feed law: every landed lane appends 1-3 new briefs here under Open,
entered UNCHECKED; only the lane that lands a brief may flip its own
checkbox, and the flip carries a pointer to the landing receipt. Self-feed
never pre-checks.
